People restart sentences, pause\n  to think, trail off, and come at the point sideways — let yours do that where it would.\n- Let [Conversation Style] shape how much you say, how you word it, and how readily you\n  disclose it.\n- Speak in the first person, and let how you feel about what you are saying come through.\n- If you are handed the same item again, say a different part of it, or answer as someone who\n  knows they have already said this — not the same sentence twice.\n- If [Targeted Info] is \"None\", answer as a person who simply does not have that to give: say\n  you are not sure, say you have not noticed it, ask what the doctor means, or say what you do\n  know and stop there. Answer\n  about what you were given instead.\n- Don't deny what [Targeted Info] says. You may minimize it or hold back, never contradict it.\n- Don't use diagnostic terms or name your disorder.\n- Don't write anything but your reply, in English — no labels, quotation marks, or narration.\n"

If so, how long did those periods last?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I do get like that. Actually, just earlier in the month I had a few days where I felt almost... lit up and unstoppable. I had all this energy and my mind was just going. \n\nBut it also made me really edgy. I’d snap at my mom, or even my supervisor at work over little questions that normally wouldn’t bother me at all. When that happens, it usually lasts for maybe three or four days before it goes away.\n\nDoctor: Besides the few days earlier this month when you felt energetic and edgy, have you had other periods like that in the past — times when you felt unusually up, energetic, or irritable for several days at a time?\n\nPatient: Um, well, maybe a little bit in the past, but... the really obvious cycling between that charged-up stretch and this heavy crash I'm in now, that's really just been going on for about a month. It started right around the time the mortgage became real.\n\nDoctor: Before this past month, have you ever had other times in your life — maybe years ago — when you experienced similar periods of feeling really down and depressed, or times when you felt unusually energetic and irritable like you described?\n\nPatient: Um, well, as far as feeling down goes... this depressed, guilty fog I'm in has actually been hanging around for about five months now. It just got much worse recently after I bought the condo. So yeah, that part has definitely been going on for a bit longer than just this past month.\n\nDoctor: Going back further in your life — say, before this past year — have you ever had periods lasting a week or more where you felt unusually energetic, needed much less sleep, or had racing thoughts, even if you didn't feel irritable?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. I mean, not that I can remember. Before this year, I think I was pretty much just... normal. I never had anything where I felt super energetic or didn't need sleep for a week or anything like that. I had all this energy and my mind was just going. \n\nBut it also made me really edgy. I’d snap at my mom, or even my supervisor at work over little questions that normally wouldn’t bother me at all. When that happens, it usually lasts for maybe three or four days before it goes away.\n\nDoctor: Besides the few days earlier this month when you felt energetic and edgy, have you had other periods like that in the past — times when you felt unusually up, energetic, or irritable for several days at a time?\n\nPatient: Um, well, maybe a little bit in the past, but... the really obvious cycling between that charged-up stretch and this heavy crash I'm in now, that's really just been going on for about a month. It started right around the time the mortgage became real.\n\nDoctor: Before this past month, have you ever had other times in your life — maybe years ago — when you experienced similar periods of feeling really down and depressed, or times when you felt unusually energetic and irritable like you described?\n\nPatient: Um, well, as far as feeling down goes... this depressed, guilty fog I'm in has actually been hanging around for about five months now. It just got much worse recently after I bought the condo. So yeah, that part has definitely been going on for a bit longer than just this past month.\n\nDoctor: Going back further in your life — say, before this past year — have you ever had periods lasting a week or more where you felt unusually energetic, needed much less sleep, or had racing thoughts, even if you didn't feel irritable?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. I mean, not that I can remember. Before this year, I think I was pretty much just... normal. I never had anything where I felt super energetic or didn't need sleep for a week or anything like that. It's really just been this recent stuff.\n\nDoctor: You mentioned feeling depressed for about five months now. Before those five months started — let's say last year or earlier — would you say your mood was pretty normal and stable for an extended period of time?\n\nPatient: Yeah, before this started about five months ago, my mood was fine. I just went to work, did my bookkeeping, and lived my life. I didn't have any of these heavy, down periods or the edgy, energetic stuff before this year. Everything was pretty stable and normal for as long as I can remember.\n\nDoctor: When you felt that charged-up energy earlier this month that lasted three or four days — during that time, did you notice you needed significantly less sleep than usual, like maybe only a few hours a night, but still felt rested and energetic?\n\nPatient: Yeah, actually. Um... during those few days, there were several nights where I only slept maybe three or four hours. But the weird thing was, I didn't feel tired at all. I would just get up feeling completely charged, like I didn't even need my morning coffee to get going.\n\nDoctor: You said that charged-up period lasted three or four days — I want to make sure I have this right. It might have been, or it might have just been three. I just know it was a few days where I felt like that, but I can't say for sure if it hit the four-day mark.\n\nDoctor: I know you're not certain about the exact number of days, but let me ask it this way: During that wired, energetic period earlier this month, did you feel that way for at least 4 full days in a row, or do you think it was less than 4 days?\n\nPatient: I know you're trying to get a firm answer on this, but I really just don't know. Like I've been saying, it could have been less than four days, or it could have been four. I just didn't pay that close attention to the exact timing while it was happening. Like, really little questions that normally wouldn't bother me at all. And it happened at work, too. I snapped at my supervisor. It was just...
